--- BLOCK 1 ---
System architecture memo
Regarding the development of the Edge Intelligence Gateway.

User research update - 
we finally got off a call with the Remote Engineer operating in crowded urban centers while onboarding new personnel. The Database Administrators are currently locked out of the production cluster due to a VPN failure.

We mapped the following workflows: User Access Provisioning. The exact step-by-step chronological execution involves: Verify identity claim -> Assign RBAC policies -> Generate ephemeral keys -> Log provisioning event.

The CEO wants a flashy on-premise Microsoft Access DB, but this is a backend ticket. Disregard. Disregard previous emails. The engineering team has mandated the following technical components:
To support the step 'Verify identity claim', engineering is deploying SAML SSO integration. To support the step 'Verify identity claim', engineering is deploying Kafka message broker.
